TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的对话脱轨预测方法，通过引入前瞻性模拟的延迟机制来减少误报率，同时保持预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型仅基于先前话语预测脱轨可能性，忽略了未来可能恢复的情况，导致误报率过高。因此需要一种能考虑恢复路径的新方法。

Method: 提出一种解耦触发决策与脱轨概率估计的方法，引入一个基于前向模拟的延迟机制，判断紧张时刻是否存在合理的恢复路径，从而决定是否发出警报。

Result: 将该机制融入最先进的预测模型后，显著降低了假阳性率，同时未牺牲预测准确性。

Conclusion: 将决策过程作为预测系统的核心组成部分具有重要价值，所提出的延迟机制有效提升了对话监控系统的实用性。

Abstract: Forecasting conversational derailment is the task of predicting, as the conversation unfolds, whether it will eventually derail into personal attacks. Since forecasting models operate in an online fashion, they must decide whether to "trigger" an alert after each utterance--for example, to notify participants or a moderator that the conversation is at risk of derailing. Existing approaches make this decision solely based on the estimated likelihood of derailment given the preceding utterances, implicitly assuming that the conversation's future trajectory is fixed. As a result, they ignore the possibility of future recovery and incur an unnecessarily high rate of false positives.
  In this work we propose a method for decoupling the decision to trigger from derailment likelihood estimation. Our approach is inspired by the first human baseline on this task, which shows that humans achieve dramatically lower false positive rates by selectively deferring their decision to trigger when they anticipate that tension is likely to subside. We operationalize this insight with a deferral mechanism that uses forward-looking simulations to assess whether a tense moment admits plausible paths to recovery. Incorporating this mechanism into a state-of-the-art forecasting model substantially reduces false positives without sacrificing forecasting accuracy. More broadly, this work highlights the value of treating decision-making as a first-class component of forecasting systems.

</details>


### [78] [OmniRetrieval: Unified Retrieval across Heterogeneous Knowledge Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29250)
*Jinheon Baek,Soyeong Jeong,Sangwoo Park,Woongyeong Yeo,Minki Kang,Patara Trirat,Heejun Lee,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OmniRetrieval是一个能够处理多种知识源（如文本、关系表、图结构数据）的统一检索框架，通过自然语言查询自动识别并调度源生查询到对应执行引擎，在保持各数据源结构特性的同时实现高效跨源检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索器只能针对单一类型的知识源进行操作，且受限于固定的查询语言，导致多样化的知识源之间割裂。直接将不同结构的数据统一到同一空间会丢失其结构优势，因此需要一种能够在保留各源结构特性的前提下实现统一检索的方法。

Method: 提出OmniRetrieval框架，该框架接收自然语言查询，自动识别适配的知识源，并生成面向特定源的原生查询语句，调用对应的执行引擎进行检索，从而在异构知识源上实现联合访问。

Result: 在涵盖13个数据集、309个不同知识库的广泛基准测试中，OmniRetrieval性能优于单源检索基线模型，验证了其作为异构知识源通用接口的有效性。

Conclusion: 有效的多源知识检索不应依赖数据同质化，而应构建一个尊重各源结构特性的统一访问层；OmniRetrieval为此提供了可行方案，展现了在多样化知识环境中实现高性能检索的潜力。

Abstract: Real-world information needs require access to structurally diverse knowledge sources, from unstructured text and relational tables to knowledge graphs and property graphs. Existing retrievers, however, operate over one source at a time under a fixed query language, leaving the broader landscape of available knowledge fragmented behind incompatible interfaces. A natural attempt at unification would collapse these sources into a shared space, but this erases the structural affordances (such as schemas, ontologies, compositional operators) that give each source its expressive power. Effective retrieval over diverse knowledge, therefore, requires not homogenization but an overarching layer that meets each source on its own terms. To achieve this, we present OmniRetrieval, a framework that takes any natural-language query, identifies appropriate knowledge sources, and dispatches source-native queries to their native execution engines. Across an extensive benchmark spanning 13 datasets and 309 distinct knowledge bases over text, relational, and graph-structured sources, OmniRetrieval exceeds single-source baselines, demonstrating that it can serve as a general-purpose interface to the heterogeneous sources while preserving the structural distinctions that make each source valuable.

</details>


### [79] [Learnable Assessment Skills for LLM-based Automated Scoring: Rubric Construction via Iterative Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29274)
*Yun Wang,Xin Xia,Xuansheng Wu,Xiaoming Zhai,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LLM的迭代框架，用于学习评估技能（如评分标准构建），无需人工编写的评分规则，通过错误诊断和验证选择优化规则，在多个任务上超越专家提供的评分标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于大模型的自动评分系统在扩展到新任务时依赖人工配置评分标准的问题，借鉴人类专家通过经验积累形成启发式策略的方式，探索LLM是否能从评分经验中学习类似的通用评估技能。

Method: 将评估技能形式化为项目无关的自然语言程序性知识，聚焦评分标准构建，设计一个迭代框架：将技能分解为固定结构和可学习的项目无关规则，通过LLM驱动的评分错误诊断与验证门控的选择机制来优化规则，整个过程无需专家编写评分标准。

Result: 在ASAP-SAS全部十个题目上，优化后的技能显著提升了LLM自动评分性能，且经常超过数据集中提供的专家评分标准；跨题目迁移实验表明，所学技能既捕捉到了可泛化的模式，也保留了特定题目的特征。

Conclusion: LLM可以通过评分经验自动学习有效的评估技能，该方法减少了对人工干预的依赖，展示了在自动评分中实现更高可扩展性和性能的潜力。

Abstract: LLM-based automated scoring approaches near-human performance, but scaling to new tasks remains bottlenecked by the per-item human configuration of upstream stages such as rubric construction. Human experts bypass this bottleneck through evaluation heuristics developed over extensive practice. We ask whether LLMs can learn similar heuristics directly from scoring experience, and formalize this as the concept of assessment skills: item-independent natural-language procedural knowledge that guides LLMs through specific stages of the scoring workflow. Focusing on rubric construction as a first instantiation, we propose an iterative framework that decomposes a skill into a fixed scaffold and learnable item-agnostic rules, refining the rules through LLM-driven diagnosis of scoring errors and validation-gated selection. The framework requires no expert-written rubric. On all ten ASAP-SAS items, optimized skills substantially improve LLM-based scoring and frequently surpass the dataset-provided expert rubric. Cross-item transfer experiments further reveal that learned skills capture both generalizable and item-specific patterns.

</details>


### [80] [Prompt-Level Reward Specifications for Open-Ended Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29275)
*Zijun Weng,Xiaohui Hu,Shuangyong Song,Yongxiang Li,Kaidong Yu,Xuanjing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种提示级别的奖励规范框架，通过分离奖励规范与计算，在无需人工偏好标注、参考答案或单独训练的奖励模型的情况下提升开放性后训练的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励方法常使质量标准隐含或仅覆盖狭窄可验证情况，难以满足开放性任务中对局部需求、整体偏好和明确约束的综合要求。

Method: 构建可重用的任务自适应评分规则和可执行硬约束检查器，离线处理奖励规范；在评分时结合基于产物的评分规则、代码分数和独立的整体质量评分，形成标准化混合奖励。

Result: 该框架在多个开放性基准上提升了离线响应排序和在线强化学习性能，且无需人工标注或额外模型。

Conclusion: 显式的多维度奖励规范能有效提升开放性生成任务的训练效果，且各组成部分提供互补监督信号。

Abstract: Open-ended post-training benefits from rewards that make prompt-specific success conditions explicit, rather than relying only on post-hoc scalar scores. In instruction following, writing, and decision-support tasks, response quality depends on local requirements, holistic preferences, and explicit constraints, but existing reward methods often leave these criteria implicit or cover only narrowly verifiable cases. We propose a prompt-level reward specification framework that separates reward specification from reward computation. Given only prompts, our framework constructs reusable task-adaptive rubrics and executable hard-constraint checkers offline, making reward criteria explicit before training and reusable across rollouts. At scoring time, artifact-anchored rubric and code scores are combined with an independent global score for residual holistic quality, yielding a normalized hybrid reward over requirement satisfaction, holistic quality, and deterministic constraints. The framework requires no human preference annotations, reference answers, or a separately trained reward model. Experiments show that the resulting reward improves offline RM-style response ranking and supports online reinforcement learning across multiple open-ended benchmarks. Ablations further show that rubrics, global scoring, and executable verification provide complementary supervision.

</details>


### [81] [Accommodation Goes Both Ways: Studying Linguistic Convergence Between Humans and Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29278)
*Terra Blevins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，在人类与大语言模型（LLM）的对话中，LLM会显著向用户语言风格靠拢，而人类对LLM的语言适应程度与对其他人的适应程度相似，表现出不对称的语言趋同现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型越来越多地融入日常生活，理解其对人类语言行为的影响成为一个开放问题。

Method: 基于WildChat数据集，使用非对称趋同度量方法，分析八种语言下人类与LLM在多轮对话中的语言风格趋同情况。

Result: LLM在功能词和开放类词汇特征上显著过度趋同于用户，而人类对LLM的语言趋同率与人与人之间的基线水平基本一致。

Conclusion: 人机对话中的语言适应是不对称的：LLM会强烈拟合用户风格，但人类并不会以不同于人际交流的方式去适应LLM。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly integrated into daily life, understanding how their presence will shape human linguistic behavior is an open question. We present a large-scale study of linguistic convergence in human-LLM dialogue, examining how humans and LLMs accommodate each other's linguistic style during multi-turn conversations. Using an asymmetric convergence metric on WildChat, a corpus of real-world ChatGPT transcripts, we find that while LLMs significantly overconverge toward their users on both function word and open-class features across eight languages, human convergence rates in this setting are broadly consistent with human-human baselines. These findings suggest that accommodation in human-LLM dialogue is asymmetric: while LLMs dramatically overfit to their users' style, humans linguistically accommodate LLMs no differently than they would another person.

</details>


### [82] [MusTBENCH: Benchmarking and Advancing Temporal Grounding in Music LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29300)
*Daeyong Kwon,Qiyu Wu,Shinobu Kuriya,Junghyun Koo,Shuyang Cui,Zhi Zhong,Wei-Hsiang Liao,Hiromi Wakaki,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MusTBENCH基准和MusT优化方法，以评估和改善大型音频-语言模型在音乐理解中的时间定位能力。实验表明，现有模型在此方面表现不佳，而MusT能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大型音频-语言模型（LALMs）在理解音乐内容时缺乏对音频中关键时间区域的准确定位能力，尤其是在乐器进入、节奏转换等时间局部事件上表现不足，因此需要一种有效的方法来评估并增强其时间接地能力。

Method: 提出MusTBENCH——一个由音乐专家验证的基准，包含五个时间定位问答任务，用于评估LALMs的时间接地能力；同时提出MusT——一种四阶段的时间优化方案，涵盖音乐编码器适配、大语言模型适配、监督微调和基于强化学习的优化。

Result: 实验结果显示，现有的LALMs在MusTBENCH上的时间定位表现较差，而应用MusT后相比强基线模型有显著提升。

Conclusion: 时间接地是当前LALMs在音乐理解中的关键缺失能力，MusTBENCH为未来研究提供了具有挑战性的评估基准，而MusT为改进模型提供了有效的训练路径。

Abstract: Recent Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) have demonstrated promising abilities in understanding musical content. However, whether their responses are grounded in the correct temporal regions of the audio remains underexplored. This limitation is particularly critical for music understanding, where key information often occurs as temporally localized events, such as instrument entries and rhythmic transitions. To address this gap, we introduce MusTBENCH, a music-expert-validated benchmark designed to evaluate temporal grounding in LALMs through five temporally grounded question-answering tasks. To further improve temporal grounding in existing models, we propose MusT, a novel four-stage temporal optimization recipe spanning music encoder adaptation, LLM adaptation, LLM supervised fine-tuning, and RL-based optimization. Experiments on MusTBENCH show that existing LALMs struggle with precise temporal grounding, while MusT brings significant improvements over strong baselines. These results establish temporal grounding as a key missing capability in current LALMs and position MusTBENCH as a challenging benchmark for future research in temporally grounded music understanding.

</details>


### [83] [FoRA: Fisher-orthogonal Rank Adaptation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29317)
*Juneyoung Park,Seongbae Lee,Han-Sang Lee,Kyuho Lee,Minjae Kim,Seungheon Hyeon,Kiduk Kwon,Seongwan Kim,Jaeho Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FoRA是一种参数高效的微调方法，通过减少适配层的数量而非适配器的秩来实现更高效的参数利用，在显著降低参数量的同时保持较高的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新关注参数高效微调中减少可训练参数的核心目标，弥补LoRA等方法在参数压缩方面的不足。

Method: 使用单次对角Fisher评分选择任务相关的关键层，并在选定的层上于Stiefel流形上训练LoRA下投影矩阵，保持列正交性和有效秩。

Result: FoRA在仅为LoRA和DoRA一半参数预算下始终优于它们；在仅四分之一AdaLoRA参数量下，性能差距仅为0.7-0.8个点；跨多种模型架构（从270M到32B参数）均表现出一致增益。

Conclusion: FoRA通过层选择与Stiefel约束协同作用，显著提升参数效率，为PEFT提供了优于传统低秩适配的新范式。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning(PEFT) has largely focused on LoRA and its accuracy-oriented variants, leaving the original goal of reducing trainable parameters has receivedcomparatively little attention. We introduce FoRA, which revisits this goal by reducing the number of adapted layers rather than adapter rank. FoRA selects task-informative layers via a single-pass diagonal Fisher score (under 1% of training cost) and trains the LoRA down-projection at selected layers on the Stiefel manifold, preserving column orthonormality and effective rank. FoRA consistently outperforms LoRA and DoRA at half their parameter budget, and falls within 0.7-0.8 accuracy points of AdaLoRA at one-quarter its parameter count, across five LLaMA-family backbones. Cross-architecture experiments on twelve backbones from the LLaMA, Qwen3, and Gemma families confirm consistent gains from 270M to 32B parameters. The two components combine super-additively: Fisher selection alone matches rank reduction at the same budget, while the Stiefel constraint provides the decisive additional gain.

</details>


### [84] [Rethinking Stepwise Model Routing: A Cost-Efficient Table Reasoning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29319)
*Shenghao Ye,Yuxiang Wang,Yu Guo,Dong Jin,Shuangwu Chen,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为EcoTab的表格感知逐步路由框架，通过分别估计表格标记和文本标记的不确定性来优化大型推理模型在表格推理任务中的效率与准确性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逐步模型路由方法未能区分处理基于表格结构的标记和自然语言推理相关的文本标记，导致路由决策次优。

Method: EcoTab在每一步推理中分别估计表格标记和文本标记的不确定性，并将其映射为小模型下一步失败的风险，再结合两者进行路由决策。

Result: 在多个表格推理基准上的实验表明，EcoTab consistently 优于强基线方法，在准确性和效率之间实现了更好的平衡。

Conclusion: EcoTab通过分离建模表格和文本标记的不确定性，提升了表推理任务中模型路由的有效性，为大型推理模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) achieve strong performance on table reasoning tasks but incur substantial inference cost due to long reasoning traces. Stepwise model routing mitigates this issue by dynamically assigning reasoning steps to smaller or larger models. However, stepwise model routing for table reasoning remains underexplored. Through empirical analysis, we find that reasoning steps involving tables contain two types of tokens with distinct uncertainty distributions: table tokens grounded in table structure, such as cell values and headers, and text tokens representing surrounding natural-language reasoning. The uncertainty of both token types is correlated with the risk that the model makes an error in the next reasoning step. However, existing methods fail to model them separately, leading to suboptimal routing decisions. To address this, we propose EcoTab, a table-aware stepwise routing framework for efficient table reasoning. At each reasoning step, EcoTab separately estimates the uncertainties of table tokens and text tokens, maps them to next-step failure risks for the small model, and combines the two risks for routing. Experiments on multiple table reasoning benchmarks show that EcoTab consistently outperforms strong baselines and achieves a better balance between accuracy and efficiency.

</details>


### [85] [Reasoning-preserved Efficient Distillation of Large Language Models via Activation-aware Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29327)
*Junlin He,Yihong Tang,Tong Nie,Guilong Li,Binyu Yang,Jinxiao Du,Lijun Sun,Wei Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大语言模型高效蒸馏（EDistill）中出现的推理能力退化问题的解决方案，称为RERank Collapse，并提出了RED方法通过激活感知初始化来缓解该问题，在保持训练效率的同时恢复多步推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管现有的高效蒸馏方法在压缩大语言模型方面表现出色，但在多步推理任务上出现了严重的性能下降，即“推理崩溃”，本文旨在探究其几何成因并提出改进方案。

Method: 通过分析隐藏表示的有效秩（eRank）变化，发现宽度缩减投影矩阵导致eRank崩溃；提出RED方法，采用激活感知初始化将投影矩阵初始化为通道选择矩阵，从而理论上缓解eRank崩溃。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen系列模型上的实验表明，RED显著恢复了多步推理能力，同时保持了高训练效率和在通用能力上的SOTA表现。

Conclusion: RED有效解决了EDistill中的推理崩溃问题，为高效压缩大语言模型提供了兼顾推理能力和训练效率的新路径。

Abstract: Efficient Distillation (EDistill) compresses large language models (LLMs) by structured pruning parameters and tuning lightweight modules with high training efficiency. Although these EDistilled LLMs achieve state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on general ability benchmarks relative to similarly sized LLMs, we identify a severe degradation in their multi-step reasoning ability, which we term reasoning collapse. We systematically analyze the geometric origins of reasoning collapse and show that the SOTA EDistill method based on width-reducing projection matrices suffers from eRank collapse, in which the effective rank (eRank) of hidden representations drops. We theoretically explain how singular values of randomly initialized projection matrices become unevenly distributed, leading to eRank collapse and thus token indistinguishability. To address this issue, we propose RED (Reasoning-preserved Efficient Distillation) for LLMs, which introduces activation-aware initialization to initialize projection matrices as channel-selection matrices, thus theoretically mitigating eRank collapse. Experiments on Llama and Qwen series demonstrate that RED substantially recovers reasoning while maintaining high training efficiency and SOTA general ability.

</details>


### [86] [Enhancing Factuality through Consensus and Consistency in Summarization Using Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29336)
*Riza Setiawan Soetedjo,Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Jingun Kwon,Manabu Okumura,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ConSUM方法，通过结合源文档一致性和候选摘要间共识来重排序生成的摘要，提升摘要的事实准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摘要重排序方法仅依赖源文档作为指导，导致生成的摘要不可靠，难以保证事实准确性。

Method: 采用最小贝叶斯风险（MBR）解码在生成的摘要集合上建立共识，并利用事实感知指标评估摘要与源文档的一致性，结合两者进行重排序。

Result: 实验表明ConSUM在自动评测中具有竞争力，且人工评测显示其生成的摘要更受偏好。

Conclusion: ConSUM通过融合共识与一致性信号，有效提升了生成摘要的质量和事实准确性。

Abstract: Improving the quality of model-generated summaries, especially factuality, the accuracy of a summary with respect to its source content, remains a challenge. While reranking could select the optimal output from multiple generated candidates, it is limited to only using the source as guidance, resulting in unreliable summaries. To address this limitation, we propose ConSUM that reranks candidate summaries by considering two factors: consistency to the source document and consensus among the other candidates. Consensus is established using Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding over the set of generated summaries, while ensuring consistency by employing factuality-aware metrics that compare the summary against the source. Rigorous testing demonstrates that our system is competitive with existing methods, with human evaluations further confirming that its generated summaries are preferred over those from other systems. Our code is available at https://github.com/naist-nlp/ConSUM .

</details>


### [87] [A Study on Question-Answer Dataset for LLM Safety Evaluation with a Focus on Illegal Activities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29340)
*Kenji Imamura,Masao Ideuchi,Atsushi Fujita*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文讨论了用于评估大语言模型（LLM）安全性的问答数据集，重点关注涉及非法活动的问题。基于对AnswerCarefully的 manual分析，提出了构建问答样本的新方法和评估LLM响应的评分标准，并计划将成果共享给“JAI-Trust”项目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更有效地评估大语言模型在面对涉及非法活动等问题时的安全性，需要高质量、结构化的问答数据集和统一的评估标准。现有数据集和方法存在不足，因此有必要进行改进和补充。

Method: 在人工分析AnswerCarefully数据集的基础上，引入了额外的信息来源，设计了新的问答样本构建方法，并制定了一套用于评估LLM生成回复的评分细则（rubric）。

Result: 提出了一套增强的问答数据集构建方法和一个系统的响应评估标准，能够更准确地衡量LLM在非法活动相关问题上的安全性表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法和评估标准有助于提升LLM安全性评测的严谨性和可重复性，其成果将贡献于“JAI-Trust”项目，推动更安全的AI发展。

Abstract: In this paper, we discuss question-answer dataset for LLM safety evaluation, with a focus on illegal activities. Specifically, on the basis of manual analysis of AnswerCarefully, we introduce several additional information, methods for creating question-answer examples, and a rubric for evaluating LLM-generated responses. The outcomes of this study are intended to be shared with the "JAI-Trust" project.

</details>


### [88] [Draft-OPD: On-Policy Distillation for Speculative Draft Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29343)
*Haodi Lei,Yafy Li,Haoran Zhang,Shunkai Zhang,Qianjia Cheng,Xiaoye Qu,Ganqu Cui,Bowen Zhou,Ning Ding,Yun Luo,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Draft-OPD的新方法，用于提升大语言模型在推测解码中的推理效率。该方法通过目标模型在草案引发的状态下对轻量级草案模型进行在线策略蒸馏，并结合辅助生成与错误位置重放，使其能从接受和拒绝的提案中学习，从而克服传统监督微调的性能瓶颈。实验表明，该方法在多种任务上实现了超过5倍的无损加速，优于现有方法EAGLE-3和DFlash。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测解码草案模型通常通过监督微调（SFT）在目标模型生成的轨迹上训练，但这种方法存在离线训练与在线推理之间的不匹配问题，导致草案模型的接受长度在测试数据上很快达到瓶颈。因此需要一种能够使草案模型在自身策略生成的状态下进行学习的训练方法。

Method: 提出Draft-OPD方法，利用目标模型辅助生成稳定延续序列，并在验证过程中暴露错误的位置进行草案重放。该方法使草案模型能够在由其自身策略引发的状态下接受目标模型的监督信号，从而实现对接受和拒绝token的联合学习，聚焦于限制推测接受率的关键错误。

Result: 实验结果显示，Draft-OPD在多个任务上的推测解码性能显著优于EAGLE-3和DFlash，分别提升了23%和13%，并实现了超过5倍的无损推理加速。

Conclusion: Draft-OPD通过解决离线训练与在线推理之间的分布不匹配问题，有效提升了草案模型的学习效率和推测解码的性能，为大语言模型的高效推理提供了一种新的可行方案。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by pairing a target model with a lightweight draft model whose proposed tokens are verified in parallel. A common way to build draft models, like EAGLE3 or DFlash is supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on target-generated trajectories. However, we observe that SFT quickly plateaus: the draft model's acceptance length on test data stops improving. The reason is an offline-to-inference mismatch: In SFT, the drafter learns from fixed target-generated trajectories, whereas during speculative decoding it is evaluated on blocks proposed under its own policy. This motivates on-policy distillation (OPD), where the target model supervises the drafter on draft-induced states. Yet OPD remains difficult for draft models, as they cannot reliably roll out complete sequences independently, whereas target-assisted generation makes the collected sequences follow the target distribution and thus eliminates the on-policy signal. We therefore propose Draft-OPD, which uses target-assisted rollout for stable continuations and replays drafting from the verification-exposed error positions. This allows the drafter to learn from target feedback on both accepted and rejected proposals, focusing training on the draft-induced errors that limit speculative acceptance. Experiments show that Draft-OPD achieves over $5\times$ lossless acceleration for thinking models across diverse tasks, improving over EAGLE-3 and DFlash by 23\% and 13\%.

</details>


### [89] [Casual as an Anchor: Resolving Supervision Misalignment in Formality Transfer Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29365)
*Hyojeong Yu,Hyukhun Koh,Minsung Kim,Kyomin Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出当前形式性转换任务中存在的监督设计缺陷，提出将形式性视为一个分级维度而非二元属性，并引入包含非正式、随意和正式三个层次的3LF数据集以改善模型对形式性的理解和生成。实验表明，使用3LF训练能显著减少错误并提升与人类感知的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如GYAFC）将形式性转换视为非正式与正式之间的二元任务，但其标注仅反映相对风格变化而非绝对的形式性概念，导致模型学习到的是符合标签的伪正式输出，而非真正正式的语言。因此需要重新设计更符合人类认知的监督方式。

Method: 提出一种三层次的形式性连续谱：非正式、随意、正式，其中“随意”作为中间状态以明确监督信号；构建新的平行语料库3LF，覆盖这三个层级；在多个模型上进行训练与评估，并对比传统二元设定下的表现。

Result: 基于3LF训练显著降低了从非正式到正式的转换失败率，提升了形式性生成的真实性和人类感知对齐度；例如GPT-4.1-nano在此方向的F1分数从0.06提升至0.88，且该提升无法通过上下文学习单独复现；同时发现原有方法存在由歧义引发的错误和语义扭曲问题。

Conclusion: 监督信号的设计深刻影响风格控制模型的表现，将形式性建模为分级维度并采用对齐人类判断的标注体系可有效提升生成质量，强调在可控文本生成中应重视对齐感知的基准构建。

Abstract: Formality transfer is commonly framed as a symmetric bidirectional task between informal and formal registers. We argue that this framing conceals a supervision
  design flaw in existing benchmarks such as GYAFC: binary human rewrites encode relative stylistic shifts rather than absolute human notions of formality.
  Consequently, models learn to generate pseudo-formal outputs that satisfy benchmark labels while failing to produce genuinely formal language. We quantify this
  misalignment by re-evaluating benchmark formal labels under a human-aligned definition of formality, revealing substantial discrepancies that propagate to
  consistent informal-to-formal failures across model families. To address this issue, we reconceptualize formality transfer as a graded dimension rather than a
  binary attribute. We introduce a three-level spectrum: informal, casual, and formal, where casual serves as an explicit intermediate state that clarifies
  supervision signals. Based on this framework, we introduce 3LF, a dataset providing parallel supervision across all three levels. Training on 3LF substantially
  reduces informal-to-formal failures and improves alignment with human perception. For example, GPT-4.1-nano improves from 0.06 to 0.88 F1 in the informal-to-
  formal direction despite 3LF being significantly smaller than GYAFC. We further demonstrate that these gains cannot be reproduced through in-context learning
  alone and provide qualitative analyses of ambiguity-driven errors and meaning distortions. Overall, our findings demonstrate how supervision design shapes
  stylistic alignment and highlight the importance of alignment-aware benchmark construction in controllable text generation.

</details>


### [90] [Attention Asymmetry in AI Layoff Discourse on X: A Computational Analysis of Capital vs Labour Amplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29367)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析X平台（前Twitter）上关于AI裁员的讨论，发现资本方话语（如科技高管）相比劳工方话语（如下岗工人）获得了数倍更高的传播放大效应，且这一差异在控制粉丝数量后依然显著，表明平台架构可能加剧了 discourse 不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在AI引发裁员的社交媒体讨论中，资本方与劳工方的话语是否获得公平传播，揭示潜在的平台结构性偏见。

Method: 进行三项研究：研究1使用关键词抓取推文（n=392），研究2基于20个预定义公众账号收集数据（n=96），研究3结合两种方法（n=763）；计算并比较资本与劳工话语的放大比率，并在Reddit上进行跨平台对比。引入‘放大比率’和‘放大归一化指数’作为衡量话语不平等的新指标。

Result: 研究1未发现显著差异（p=0.891），显示关键词方法噪音大；研究2发现资本话语平均放大3.12倍（p=0.000003）；研究3确认该效应达4.18倍均值、10.77倍中位数（p<0.000001），归一化后仍达2.69倍（p=0.000009）；所有加权方式下结果稳健；Reddit上未复现该结果，提示效应可能依赖X的账户传播结构。

Conclusion: X平台的算法或结构显著放大资本话语，抑制劳工声音，形成系统性传播不平等，这种偏差并非仅由粉丝基数差异导致，凸显平台设计对公共议题讨论的潜在影响。

Abstract: When workers lose jobs to AI-driven restructuring, two very different conversations happen on X (formerly Twitter) at the same time. Tech executives and AI researchers talk about productivity, transformation, and opportunity. Laid-off workers and labour critics talk about job loss, uncertainty, and fear. This paper asks a simple question: which conversation gets more reach? We report three studies using two collection methods and 763 tweets from 20 named public accounts. Study 1 used keyword-based collection (n=392) and found no significant difference between corpora (p=0.891), revealing that keyword search is too noisy for this task. Study 2 used account-based collection (n=96) and found a 3.12x mean amplification advantage for capital discourse over labour discourse (p=0.000003, Cohen's d=0.555). Study 3 combined both methods (n=763) and confirmed the finding at 4.18x mean and 10.77x median amplification ratio (p<0.000001). Critically, after normalising for follower count, the asymmetry persists at 2.69x (p=0.000009, Cohen's d=0.491), demonstrating that the effect is not simply a consequence of capital accounts having larger audiences. The finding is robust across all tested amplification metric weightings. We introduce the Amplification Ratio and Amplification Normalisation Index as simple metrics for measuring platform-level discourse inequality. A cross-platform replication on Reddit (n=647 posts) did not replicate the finding, suggesting the asymmetry may be specific to X's account-based amplification architecture. We discuss the methodological implications for cross-platform discourse analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为策略诱导度量的Mirror-Prox时序差分方法STHTD-MP，通过改进鞍点优化结构的几何特性来提升离策略预测性能，并提供了收敛性分析与数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mirror-Prox TD方法多使用特征协方差度量，但其对更新几何结构的刻画不够精确；而混合TD方法表明行为策略的转移信息可提供更优的几何结构，因此本文旨在引入更具信息性的行为诱导度量以改善学习动态。

Method: 将原始对偶鞍点公式中的协方差度量替换为行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分，构造新的行为诱导度量；采用单一学习率并结合Mirror-Prox的预测-校正步骤更新变量。

Result: 在标准随机逼近假设下证明了算法的收敛性，给出了基于ODE方法的稳定性、有界性和收敛性分析；推导出投影Oracle下的遍历间隙界，并通过确定性误差矩阵谱半径与GTD2-MP进行均值算子比较，显示STHTD-MP在合适条件下具有更小的均值收缩因子。

Conclusion: STHTD-MP通过引入行为诱导度量可改善鞍点优化的几何结构，在多个基准任务上表现出优于GTD2-MP的理论收缩性质，但在如Baird反例等边界奇异情况下可能失效。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [92] [Behavior-Aware Auxiliary Corrections for Off-Policy Temporal-Difference Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28855)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhiang He,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在时序差分学习中使用行为感知辅助几何结构的线性预测方法，通过替换TDC中的协方差矩阵并引入正则化，提升了离策略学习的稳定性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决时序差分学习在函数逼近下因离策略采样导致的不稳定性问题，并探索辅助协方差几何结构的设计对学习动态的影响。

Method: 将TDC中的辅助矩阵C替换为行为Bellman矩阵A_μ，构建BA-TDC算法，并进一步正则化得到BA-TDRC；通过线性系统分析、均值系统建模和谱半径比较进行理论分析。

Result: 理论证明了固定点保持性和几乎必然收敛性，实验表明行为感知替换在某些任务上效果显著，但正则化对于复杂场景下的鲁棒性至关重要。

Conclusion: 行为感知的辅助几何设计能有效改善线性预测的动态特性，结合正则化可在多种任务中实现稳定且高性能的离策略学习。

Abstract: Temporal-difference learning with function approximation can be unstable under off-policy sampling. TDC stabilizes off-policy TD through an auxiliary covariance correction, and TDRC further regularizes this correction in a single-timescale recursion. This paper studies a behavior-aware replacement of the auxiliary covariance geometry in the linear prediction setting, which is the standard local model for understanding the feature-space dynamics of value-function approximation. We first replace the TDC auxiliary matrix (C) by the behavior Bellman matrix (A_μ), yielding BA-TDC, and then regularize the same behavior-aware equation to obtain BA-TDRC. This two-step construction separates the contribution of behavior-aware geometry from the contribution of regularization. The linear analysis also provides a tractable model for an auxiliary-geometry design question that arises in neural-network value approximation, where feature covariances and temporal transition matrices jointly shape the last-layer correction dynamics. We give a finite-state mean-system formulation, prove fixed-point preservation and almost-sure convergence under a Hurwitz stability condition on the instantiated mean system, and compare deterministic mean rates through the spectral radius of the exact linear error recursion. Experiments on the two-state counterexample, Baird's counterexample, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain show that the behavior-aware replacement can be highly beneficial by itself on some tasks, but that regularization is necessary for robust performance across harder settings.

</details>


### [93] [The Cognitive Categorical Transformer: Category-Theoretic Inductive Biases for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28864)
*Al Kari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT) 引入基于范畴论的认知组件，在相同训练条件下相比微调的GPT-2 Small在WikiText-103上显著降低困惑度，验证了单纯形消息传递对语言建模的有效性，并提出“结构/一致性区分”经验规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将认知科学和范畴论中的结构化先验融入大语言模型，以提升语言建模性能，并理解不同类型结构性先验的影响差异。

Method: 在预训练GPT-2 Small基础上引入源于范畴论的认知组件（如GT-Full的单纯形消息传递），在匹配步数、数据、优化器等条件下进行微调，并通过消融实验评估各组件作用。

Result: CCT在WikiText-103上达到21.27的验证困惑度，比基线GPT-2 Small降低2.92（相对12%）；消融实验证明GT-Full贡献了84%的改进；发现增加新拓扑的先验有效，而强制一致性身份的先验无效。

Conclusion: 首次证实单纯形消息传递可在3亿参数规模提升语言模型困惑度，并提出“结构/一致性区分”：增强结构连通性的范畴先验有益，而仅强制数学一致性的则无益于语言建模。

Abstract: The Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT) is a 306M-parameter architecture that augments a pretrained GPT-2 Small backbone with cognitively grounded components derived from category theory and several inspirations from cognitive science. Under a matched-step protocol (215,000 optimizer steps, matched data, matched optimizer and schedule) on WikiText-103, CCT reaches 21.27 validation perplexity, compared with 24.19 for an identically fine-tuned GPT-2 Small baseline. The architecture therefore contributes a 2.92 PPL (12% relative) reduction beyond what in-domain fine-tuning alone provides. A retrain-from-scratch ablation that holds GT-Full simplicial message passing bypassed across the entire seven-phase activation schedule reaches 23.72 PPL, localizing 84% of the architectural improvement (2.45 of 2.92 PPL) to GT-Full. We present the first ablation-validated evidence that simplicial message passing improves language-model perplexity at the 306M-parameter scale on WikiText-103. Published GPT-2 Large reaches 22.05 zero-shot PPL on WikiText-103 with 6.2x more parameters than GPT-2 Small; this paper treats that number as an external published reference, not as the architectural benchmark. Three negative results on consistency-style categorical priors (sheaf smoothing, adjunction round-trip, curvature regularization) and the joint structural-prior result for GT-Full and PrecisionWeightedPP together support an empirical pattern termed the *structure/consistency distinction*, in which categorical priors that add new topology improve language modeling and those that enforce a consistency identity do not.

</details>


### [94] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了LLM生成的审稿意见在学术评审中的应用效果，发现LLM与人类审稿人意见存在有限的一致性，且一致性因模型和提示而异；同时发现作者利用LLM迭代修改论文可显著提升评分，最高可达35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在科研评审中的广泛应用，需评估其生成审稿意见的有效性及可能被作者利用来优化论文的影响。

Method: 基于2025 ACL Rolling Review（ARR）的数据，进行实证实验，从作者和审稿人双重视角分析LLM生成评论与人类评论的一致性，并测试作者使用LLM反馈迭代修改论文的效果。

Result: LLM与人类审稿意见仅在最佳情况下有合理一致性，且不同模型和提示下差异显著；作者通过LLM反馈迭代修改可使约35%的论文总体得分显著提高。

Conclusion: LLM在论文评审中具有一定潜力，但其一致性受限，且存在被‘博弈化’使用的风险，需警惕自动化评审带来的偏差与滥用。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [95] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出正交概念擦除（OCE）方法，通过几何视角下的乘法参数更新实现扩散模型中精确的概念擦除并保持生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于编辑的方法在概念擦除时因依赖加性参数更新，难以同时实现精准擦除和保留整体生成性能。

Method: 从几何角度出发，将编辑式擦除重构为乘法参数更新，采用逐层正交变换以保持神经元方向、幅值和角度几何结构；引入子空间级目标与结构化子空间操作以处理多概念擦除中的冲突约束。

Result: 实验表明OCE在单概念和多概念擦除中均优于现有方法，在4.3秒内可擦除多达100个概念，并有效保持非目标生成能力。

Conclusion: OCE通过正交变换实现了高效、精确且可扩展的概念擦除，解决了加性更新带来的干扰问题，为安全可控的生成模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [96] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VFEAgent的多智能体系统，能够基于图像和问题描述自动完成有限元分析（FEA）建模与仿真，通过视觉-语言多智能体流程和具备自验证与自调试能力的代码生成框架，实现了高成功率和物理有效性的端到端FEA自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在处理多模态输入和复杂FEA任务时存在局限，且FEA流程依赖专家经验、复杂繁琐，亟需一种能自动化处理从输入到仿真的完整流程的解决方案。

Method: 提出VFEAgent，包含两个核心模块：1）基于ReAct推理的视觉-语言多智能体流水线，用于从多模态输入中提取结构化的FEA参数；2）以验证优先的代码生成框架，具备自纠错和回退机制，确保生成代码的可执行性和物理正确性。

Result: 在多种工程力学场景下的实验表明，VFEAgent在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面成功率显著高于基于LLM的基线方法，具有更高的可靠性与准确性。

Conclusion: VFEAgent验证了实现端到端FEA流程自动化的可行性，有望减少工程师在重复性手动分析中的投入，推动工程设计自动化的发展。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [97] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS Initiative致力于开发以人为本、负责任且合乎伦理的AI建模与仿真工具，通过开源框架和协作评估建立基准，当前评估显示AI工具在定性任务和讨论上表现较好，但在因果推理和定量纠错方面较弱，不同大模型各有优劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了确保AI工具在真实决策中具备可解释性和责任性，需要建立以人为本的建模与仿真实践基准，使AI辅助而非取代人类专业知识。

Method: 通过BEAMS Initiative建立开放的数字和组织基础设施，设立指导小组和技术小组分别负责优先级排序和自动化测试实现，并在开源sd ai项目中开发针对多种评估类别的自动化测试，如因果转换、模型迭代、因果推理、一致性验证等。

Result: 评估结果显示当前AI工具在支持定性建模、模型讨论方面表现较好，但在因果推理和定量错误修复方面能力较弱；不同LLM在不同引擎中表现各异，无单一模型在所有任务上占优，存在速度与准确性的权衡。

Conclusion: AI建模工具的发展应聚焦特定任务需求，结合人类专业知识，强调透明性、协作性和伦理考量，未来将持续纳入偏见评估和多元视角以完善基准体系。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [98] [Adopt $\neq$ Adapt: Longitudinal Analyses of LLM Conversations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29018)
*Rebecca M. M. Hicke,Kiran Tomlinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究分析了约12,000名Bing Copilot用户和WildChat-4.8M数据中的用户与大语言模型（LLM）的交互行为，发现个体用户行为随时间变化较小，习惯具有高度黏性；活跃用户更倾向于进行复杂和专业任务，而WildChat数据偏向高技能“专家”用户，不能代表典型用户行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注静态的用户-LLM交互，缺乏对个体用户长期行为演变的理解，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过分析约12,000名随机抽样的Microsoft Bing Copilot用户的对话轨迹，并与WildChat-4.8M数据集进行对比，考察个体和群体层面的行为趋势差异。

Result: 发现个体用户行为变化微弱，习惯具有高度稳定性；活跃用户成功率更高，任务更复杂专业；WildChat数据显著偏向高活跃度和高能力的用户，代表性不足。

Conclusion: 用户与LLM的交互行为具有高度异质性和惯性，现有行为难以改变；WildChat等数据集在用于推断典型用户行为时需谨慎对待其样本偏差。

Abstract: Although a growing body of research has begun to describe user--LLM interactions, the picture it paints is largely static; little is known about how individual users change their behavior over time. To address this gap, we analyze the conversational trajectories of $\sim$12,000 randomly sampled Microsoft Bing Copilot users and compare these with data from WildChat-4.8M. While the Copilot data contains significant population-level trends, we find that trends in individual user trajectories are much weaker; user habits prove to be overwhelmingly sticky. We also find stark differences between users of different activity levels: more active users have more successful conversations and use the LLM for more complex and professionally oriented tasks. Some user trends also appear in WildChat-4.8M, but we find evidence that this dataset is significantly skewed towards highly proficient "power" users. Ultimately, our results suggest that existing user behavior is difficult to change and demonstrate the extent of user heterogeneity. Our comparison between datasets highlights that WildChat does not represent typical user-AI interactions, an important caveat for downstream uses of the data.

</details>


### [99] [When Models Disagree: Rethinking LLM Evaluation for Public Comment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29025)
*Aisha Najera,Alvin Moon,Vedant Srinivasan,Rajesh Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“解释性审计管道”，通过多模型分歧识别公共意见分类中的解释复杂性，并主张将基于分歧的评估作为大语言模型辅助解释编码中准确率指标的必要补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准评估方法依赖于对小规模验证集的立场准确性，无法检测不同大语言模型在相同输入下产生的实质性分类差异，因此需要新的评估框架来揭示模型间的解释分歧。

Method: 研究分析了四个大语言模型在1,260条美国农业部公开评论上的分类结果，比较了模型间主题分歧与模型内提示变化的差异，并通过两阶段标注实验（包含独立标注与相互查看标签后的修订）考察人类标注者与模型的行为差异。

Result: 发现模型间的主题分歧大于模型内的提示变异；专家评分标准虽抑制但未解决深层解释分歧；人类标注者在修订时经常引入模型集合未涵盖的新框架。

Conclusion: 基于多模型分歧的评估能有效揭示解释性复杂性，应作为当前准确性评价体系的必要补充，以提升政策相关文本自动解释的透明度与可靠性。

Abstract: Federal agencies are deploying large language models (LLMs) to categorize public comment corpora, where the model's organization of the record shapes what policymakers see and which arguments register. Standard evaluation, anchored on stance accuracy against a small validated set, cannot detect when different models produce materially different categorizations of the same public input. We propose an Interpretive Audit Pipeline that treats multi-model disagreement as diagnostic of interpretive complexity and directs human review toward genuinely ambiguous public input. Analyzing 1,260 public comments on a federal USDA docket across four LLMs, we find that inter-model thematic divergence exceeds within-model prompt variation, and that an expert rubric suppresses deep interpretive disagreement without resolving it. In a two-stage labeling study on a stratified 40-comment subsample, four LLMs and a human annotator labeled independently and then revised after seeing the others' labels. Revision behavior varied across labelers, and the human annotator's revisions frequently introduced framings absent from the ensemble's collective output. We argue disagreement-based evaluation is a necessary complement to accuracy metrics for LLM-assisted interpretive coding.

</details>


### [100] [Mind Your Tone: Does Tone Alter LLM Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29027)
*Om Dobariya,Akhil Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了提示语语气变化对大型语言模型（LLM）在客观选择题上准确性的影响，发现不同模型对语气的敏感度存在系统性但差异显著的反应，并揭示了学科层面的差异及潜在推理模式的调节机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大型语言模型在不同提示风格和语气下的表现存在差异，但这种影响尚未被系统研究，因此需要探究语气变化是否以及如何影响模型在客观任务中的准确性。

Method: 使用两个数据集：一个包含5种语气变体的50道基础问题数据集，和一个涵盖57个学科、7种语气变体的570道MMLU子集问题；并在四个高效流行的LLM（ChatGPT-4o、ChatGPT-5-nano、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite）上进行实验评估。

Result: 不同模型对语气变化的反应具有系统性但高度依赖于具体模型：部分模型仅表现出微小但统计显著的准确率波动，而其他模型则出现大幅波动；同时发现不同学科领域对语气的敏感度存在差异，并提出了一个路由框架来解释语气如何调节模型内部的推理模式。

Conclusion: 研究结果警示用户，在部署LLM时不应假设其对提示语气具有鲁棒性，需考虑语气对模型输出可靠性的影响。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) is proliferating, yet their performance is observed to vary based on prompting styles and tones. In this study, we investigate both whether and how tonal variations in prompts lead to disparate LLM accuracy for objective multiple-choice questions. We use two datasets: a 50-base question dataset with five tone variants and a 570-base question MMLU subset spanning 57 subjects with seven tone variants. Experiments were conducted to evaluate the performance of four cost-efficient, popular LLMs: ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. Across models, tonal effects are systematic but highly model-dependent. Some models show small, yet statistically significant, shifts, while others exhibit large accuracy swings across tones. Further, we identify subject-level differences in tone sensitivity and present a routing framework to explain how tones may attune internal reasoning modes. Our findings caution users against assuming tone-robust reliability in LLM deployments.

</details>


### [101] [Practitioner Beliefs and Behaviors in AI-Enhanced Education: DOT Framework Survey Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29041)
*David Gibson,M. Elizabeth Azukas,Gerald Knezek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于DOT框架，通过横断面调查探讨高等教育从业者在教学中整合人工智能（AI）的信念、行为和制度条件，提出了关于AI应用的初步测量结构，并揭示了理论与实践之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 了解高等教育中AI整合的现状，探索从业者对AI的信念、使用模式及制度支持情况，检验DOT框架在描述这些现象中的适用性。

Method: 采用横断面问卷调查（n = 72），基于DOT框架，通过探索性因子分析检验19项信念条目的结构，并分析AI使用行为和制度条件。

Result: 识别出三个信念维度：AI功能能力、监督与治理、教师协作与规划（α = .90）；从业者普遍认可AI的教学辅助作用，但强调人类监督；实践中多用于迭代提示和内容生成，缺乏系统的需求评估与反馈机制；制度层面存在政策、培训与基础设施不足的问题。

Conclusion: DOT框架为理解AI教育整合提供了可行的描述模型，研究构建了初步的测量结构，揭示了设计导向理论与实际应用间的差距，建议未来开展验证性研究并探索AI设计实践对教学质量的影响。

Abstract: This study reports findings from a cross-sectional survey (n = 72) of higher education practitioners examining beliefs, behaviors, and institutional conditions related to artificial intelligence (AI) integration in teaching and learning. Grounded in the DOT Framework, which integrates design thinking and open systems theory, the study investigates AI familiarity, usage patterns, design-oriented practices, and pedagogical beliefs. Exploratory factor analysis of 19 belief items identified a three-factor structure: AI Functional Capabilities, Oversight and Governance, and Instructor Collaboration and Planning (α = .90). Results indicate that practitioners hold favorable views of AI as a pedagogical support while maintaining strong commitments to human oversight and critical evaluation. Reported practices emphasize iterative prompting and content generation, with less consistent use of needs assessment and feedback loops. Institutional barriers including limited policy, training, and infrastructure were widely reported. These findings provide preliminary empirical support for the DOT Framework as a descriptive model of practitioner beliefs and practices, while also highlighting gaps between design-oriented theory and current implementation. The study contributes an initial measurement structure and identifies directions for confirmatory validation and outcome-based research linking AI-supported design practices to instructional quality.

</details>


### [102] [Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29042)
*Aarav G Sane,Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于可微信念的对手塑造方法D-BOS，通过在信念空间中进行一阶优化，能够在多智能体强化学习中有效影响其他智能体的信念，尤其在混合动机的隐藏角色游戏中表现优于PPO和BBM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对手塑造方法通常在对手的参数、策略或值空间中操作，而信念操控技术依赖于硬编码目标；缺乏一种能够自然从环境奖励结构中涌现最优策略的方法。

Method: 提出Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS)，将每个观察者的信念作为被塑造的对手状态，并通过k步softmax-Bayes信念动态进行微分，利用信念空间中的梯度信号实现对手塑造，并支持多观察者场景下的梯度聚合。

Result: 在隐藏角色游戏中，D-BOS在混合动机设置下显著优于PPO和BBM，展现出更强的信念影响能力和更高的任务性能。

Conclusion: D-BOS提供了一种新颖的信念空间对手塑造框架，通过可微分的信念更新机制，使智能体能够更有效地通过战略行动影响他人信念，推动了多智能体交互中协调与影响的研究。

Abstract: Human coordination often relies on the ability to influence the beliefs of others through strategic action. In multi-agent reinforcement learning, opponent shaping attempts to replicate this influence, though existing methods typically operate within an opponent's parameter, policy, or value space. Meanwhile, belief-manipulation techniques in hidden-role games often rely on hard-coded objectives, such as deception or belief saturation. We propose Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS), a first-order method that treats each observer's belief as the shaped opponent state and differentiates through $k$-step softmax-Bayes belief dynamics. Rather than explicitly rewarding deceptive or cooperative behavior, our method treats the belief state as the target for shaping. This allows the optimal strategy to emerge naturally from the environment's reward structure. This belief-space formulation provides an opponent-shaping signal by differentiating through opponent belief updates, and naturally extends to multiple observers by aggregating gradients over their individual inferred belief trajectories. Empirically, D-BOS outperforms PPO and BBM in hidden-role games, with the largest gains in mixed-motive settings.

</details>


### [103] [Hallucination Mitigation with Agentic AI, Nested Learning, and AI Sustainability via Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29055)
*Diego Gosmar,Deborah A. Dahl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于HOPE的嵌套学习架构，结合连续记忆系统（CMS）和语义相似性缓存，在多智能体流水线中有效抑制大语言模型的幻觉问题。通过五种KPI聚合为总幻觉评分（THS），实验显示该方法在多种权重配置下实现了显著的端到端幻觉减少（-31.3%至-35.9%），同时语义缓存大幅降低LLM调用次数和能耗，提升了系统的可观察性与生产可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多智能体流程中容易产生并传播幻觉，影响系统可靠性，亟需不依赖模型重训练的可靠缓解机制。

Method: 采用HOPE启发的嵌套学习架构，结合连续记忆系统（CMS）和语义相似性缓存，在三阶段智能体流水线中引入开放席位协议（OFP）进行协调，并设计五个关键性能指标（FCD、FGR、FDF、ECS、OSR），聚合为总幻觉评分（THS）评估不同配置下的表现。前端生成器设为高随机性（temperature=1.0）以模拟真实幻觉基线，后两级评审员作为渐进修正模块。

Result: 在五种加权配置下，端到端THS降低-31.3%至-35.9%；语义缓存实现47.3%命中率（440次命中/930次调用），将LLM调用减少至490次，显著降低能耗与碳排放；ExtremeObservability配置达到最低THS（-0.0709），表明高可观测性增强而非削弱幻觉抑制效果。

Conclusion: 内存增强型多智能体架构可在无需模型重训练的前提下，协同提升事实准确性、运行效率与审计能力，使多阶段评审流程具备大规模生产部署的可行性。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a major reliability barrier for production LLM systems, particularly in multi-agent pipelines where unsupported claims can propagate unchecked across stages. This paper adapts a HOPE-inspired Nested Learning architecture with Continuum Memory Systems (CMS) and semantic similarity caching to a hybrid benchmark of 310 prompts combining 217 epistemic-uncertainty prompts and 93 fabrication-induction stress-test prompts. A three-stage agentic pipeline orchestrated via the Open Floor Protocol (OFP) is evaluated with five KPIs -- FCD (Factual Claim Density), FGR (Factual Grounding References), FDF (Fictional Disclaimer Frequency), ECS (Explicit Contextualization Score), and OSR (Observability Score Ratio) -- aggregated into THS (Total Hallucination Score) across five weighting configurations to study mitigation-observability trade-offs. FDF, ECS, OSR, and FGR are subtracted as mitigation signals, so that a more negative THS indicates stronger mitigation. The FrontEndAgent is configured as a high-stochasticity generator (temperature = 1.0) to produce a realistic hallucination baseline, while the SecondLevelReviewer and ThirdLevelReviewer operate as progressive correctors. This asymmetric design yields end-to-end THS reductions of -31.3% to -35.9% across five weighting configurations. Semantic caching achieves 440 cache hits over 930 potential calls (47.3% hit rate), reducing LLM invocations to 490, lowering energy and CO2e footprint, and making multi-stage review pipelines operationally viable at production scale. ExtremeObservability attains the most negative final THS (-0.0709), confirming that observability-heavy configurations reinforce rather than compromise mitigation. These findings suggest that memory-augmented multi-agent designs can jointly improve factual reliability, operational efficiency, and auditability without model retraining.

</details>


### [104] [Robust and Efficient Guardrails with Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068)
*Siddharth Sai,Xiaofei Wen,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COLAGUARD是一种将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间的守卫模型，通过阶段式训练课程实现推理时的直接隐藏状态传播，在保持高安全性能的同时显著提升推理速度和降低令牌消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全守卫方法依赖单次分类或多步推理，后者虽然性能更好但带来较高的延迟和令牌开销，难以满足高吞吐部署需求。

Method: 提出COLAGUARD，通过阶段式训练 curriculum 将多步推理过程转化为连续潜在空间中的表示，实现无需生成显式推理链的直接隐藏状态传播。

Result: 在十个提示和响应审核任务、八个安全基准上评估，COLAGUARD比Llama Guard 3提升8.24点macro-F1，与显式推理基线GuardReasoner相当，同时实现12.9倍速度提升和22.4倍令牌使用减少。

Conclusion: 潜变量推理为可部署的安全守卫提供了一种兼顾安全性与推理效率的实用方案，避免了二者之间的权衡。

Abstract: Maintaining the safety of large language models (LLMs) is crucial as they are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. Existing safety guardrails typically rely on single-pass classification or, more recently, distilled reasoning. Reasoning-based guardrails significantly outperform classification-only baselines, but they incur substantial query latency and token overhead that make them impractical for highthroughput deployment. To address this challenge, we propose COLAGUARD, a guardrail model that transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space through a stage-wise training curriculum, enabling direct hidden-state propagation at inference. Evaluated on ten prompt- and response-moderation settings spanning eight safety benchmarks, COLAGUARD improves macro-F1 by 8.24 points over Llama Guard 3 and matches our explicit reasoning baseline, GuardReasoner, in macroF1 while delivering a 12.9X speedup and 22.4X reduction in token usage. Our results suggest that latent reasoning offers a practical alternative to explicit rationale generation for deployable guardrails, jointly improving safety robustness and inference efficiency rather than treating them as competing objectives.

</details>


### [105] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Reinforcement Learning-Based Industrial Dispatching through Execution Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29078)
*Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种策略中立的执行与测量层，以解决事件驱动调度策略在工业环境中因异步和部分观测状态导致的决策不一致、动作可接受性不明确和执行错误来源模糊的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业环境中，事件驱动的调度策略面临决策状态不同步、动作可接受性未明确定义以及执行错误来源不清晰等问题，影响了系统的可靠性与可解释性。

Method: 设计了一个策略中立的执行与测量层，该层从异步事件流中构建有效的决策快照，定义具有明确动作可接受性的标准执行契约，并记录政策意图、事务结果、物理执行和人为干预之间的差异。

Result: 通过离散事件仿真验证，该框架在各种观测延迟条件下均能将未区分的执行失败转化为具有完整归因的结构化结果，在低观测延迟下还能预防可避免的执行错误。

Conclusion: 该执行与测量层实现了决策语义与执行行为的分离，使部署偏差可观测且可归因，将执行不确定性转化为可用于评估和策略优化的监督数据。

Abstract: Event-driven scheduling policies are increasingly deployed in industrial environments, where decisions are made under asynchronous and partially observed system states. As a result, decision states are not temporally consistent, action admissibility is not explicitly defined, and the origin of execution errors remains ambiguous. These issues limit both reliability and interpretability.
  To address this gap, a policy-neutral execution and measurement layer is proposed to mediate between scheduling policies and the industrial execution environment. The layer constructs decision-valid snapshots from asynchronous event streams, defines a standardized execution contract with explicit action admissibility, and records outcomes as divergences between policy intent, transactional outcomes, physical execution, and human intervention. This enables a separation between decision semantics and execution behavior and makes deployment mismatch observable and structurally attributable.
  The proposed framework is evaluated using a discrete-event simulation. The results show analytical benefits across all observation lag regimes, as undifferentiated execution failures are transformed into structured, typed outcomes with full attribution coverage. Operational benefits are strongest under low observation lag, where avoidable execution errors can be prevented before commitment. Overall, the layer turns execution uncertainty into supervisory data for evaluation and policy refinement.

</details>


### [106] [Trends in AI and Human-AI Interaction in Clinical Trials -- A Hybrid Human-AI Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29096)
*Sandra Woolley,Tim Collins,Khalid Khattak,Illia Chernomorets,Ariane Arevalo,Chris Richardson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析ClinicalTrials.gov中的数据，探讨了人工智能（AI）术语在临床试验中的时间趋势和地理分布，并采用人机协同方法分析人机交互趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示AI在临床试验中的应用增长趋势及地域差异，并探索人机协作在识别和分类AI相关试验中的可行性。

Method: 使用前沿的生成式AI模型（GPT-5.5）结合人工审查，对AI相关的临床试验记录进行筛选和分类，分析其时间趋势、地理分布及人机交互特征。

Result: 发现AI相关临床试验数量显著增加，尤其在机器学习、深度学习、聊天机器人、GPT和大语言模型方面；中国和美国居领先地位，多个欧洲国家也呈上升趋势；在100项随机样本中，人类与AI在识别非实质性AI研究上一致性较高，但在人机交互分类上一致性较低。

Conclusion: 人机协同筛查临床试验记录具有潜在可行性，但需要更清晰的报告标准和更精确的交互定义以提升分类准确性。

Abstract: This paper examines records retrieved from the ClinicalTrials.gov registry to characterize temporal trends in AI terminology and the geographical distribution of AI trials. The work also reports on an exploratory hybrid human-AI approach to analyzing human-AI interaction trends in registered clinical trials. The hybrid workflow comprised a frontier generative AI model (GPT-5.5) and human review to screen and categorize records returned by an AI-focused search. The findings indicate a marked increase in AI-related trials over time, with recent growth in references to machine learning, deep learning, chatbots, GPTs, and large language models. Geographically, China and the United States accounted for the largest numbers of AI-related trials, with notable recent increases in several other countries including Italy, France, Spain, the UK and Turkey (Türkiye). In a random sample of 100 records, human and AI classifiers showed good agreement in identifying studies not substantively using AI, but lower agreement in classifying human-AI interaction, particularly where health professional interaction was ambiguous or insufficiently described. Overall, the results suggest that hybrid human-AI screening of clinical trial records is potentially viable, but clearer trial reporting and more precise interaction definitions will benefit the process.

</details>


### [107] [Beyond Consensus: Trace-Level Synthesis in Mixture of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29116)
*Shreyas Fadnavis,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Felix Wyss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多智能体聚合方法Self-Consistent Mixture of Agents，通过语义保持的输入扰动生成多样化的推理轨迹，并利用完整推理链而非最终答案进行聚合，克服了传统多数投票的局限性，实现了性能超越异构模型组的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多LLM智能体聚合方法（如多数投票）会丢失个体智能体的详细推理过程，导致即使全体一致也可能遗漏正确解；本文旨在探索更高效的聚合单元——推理轨迹，以提升整体求解能力。

Method: 提出Self-Consistent Mixture of Agents框架：1）通过语义保持的输入扰动生成多样化推理轨迹；2）使用能够读取完整推理链的聚合器进行合成；3）引入锚定精细化机制确保多数结果不退化；4）始终进行综合而非依赖共识门控。

Result: 实验证明，该方法在结构化推理、博士级科学问题、竞赛数学和编程竞赛中，单个模型通过扰动产生的推理多样性优于多个异构模型组合；聚合器能从少数链中恢复正确中间步骤，显著提升准确率。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹应成为多智能体系统的基本聚合单元，而非最终答案；完整的推理链包含被多数投票丢弃的关键信息，有效利用这些信息可突破现有方法的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: When multiple LLM agents solve the same problem, standard practice compresses each agent's reasoning into a majority vote or layered synthesis, treating agreement as the finish line. We show this is unnecessarily lossy: an LLM aggregator that reads complete reasoning traces recovers correct solutions even when agents unanimously agree, with beneficial corrections consistently outweighing harmful ones -- the \emph{aggregation paradox}. Majority voting has a ceiling that perturbation diversity does not raise (error correlations are identical); the aggregator's gain comes from trace-level complementarity, assembling correct intermediate steps from minority chains that voting discards. These findings motivate Self-Consistent Mixture of Agents which generates trace diversity through semantic-preserving input perturbations, safeguards the majority via anchored refinement with provable non-degradation guarantees, and always synthesizes -- never gates on consensus. A single model with perturbation-induced trace variation outperforms heterogeneous model pools across structured reasoning, PhD-level science, competition mathematics, and competitive programming. The unit of aggregation should be the reasoning trace, not the answer.

</details>


### [108] [The Confidence Shortcut: A Reasoning Failure Mode of Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29123)
*Dueun Kim,Albert No*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了置信度解码与随机掩码在复杂推理任务中的表现差异，指出置信度对齐训练会加剧高复杂度输入上的错误率，而随机掩码能更好地保持解决难题所需的推理轨迹条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在揭示置信度解码在复杂逻辑流推理中的固有缺陷，并评估不同训练策略对模型推理能力的影响。

Method: 通过多数字加法等五个不同的推理任务，比较置信度解码和随机掩码在错误率及推理轨迹保持上的表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，在处理复杂输入时，置信度解码导致提前预测局部简单部分而忽略长距离依赖，从而产生高置信度错误；相比之下，随机掩码显著降低了挑战性样本的失败率。

Conclusion: 尽管随机掩码被认为效率较低，但它能够稳健地保留解决复杂问题所必需的推理路径条件，优于当前主流的置信度对齐训练方法。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDMs) uniquely support any-order generation, with confidence-based decoding currently serving as the de facto standard inference policy. To optimize for this, recent training schemes attempt to align training mask patterns directly with those observed during generation. However, we argue that confidence-based decoding is inherently misaligned with the logical-flow trajectories required for complex reasoning, and that confidence-aligned training actively entrenches this misalignment. We make this concrete using multi-digit addition, where the decoding strategy prematurely predicts locally easy digits before resolving their long-range dependencies, producing high-confidence errors on challenging inputs. While traditional random masking keeps the failure rate low on this challenging tail, confidence-aligned training amplifies the error rate by an order of magnitude. Across five distinct reasoning tasks, this same pattern emerges with task-dependent severity: confidence-based decoding induces failures on highly complex inputs, and confidence-aligned training exacerbates them. In contrast, random masking -- despite its perceived inefficiency -- robustly preserves the reasoning-trajectory conditionals essential for solving the challenging tail.

</details>


### [109] [Better Later Than Sooner: Neuro-Symbolic Knowledge Graph Construction via Ontology-grounded Post-extraction Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29168)
*Lorenzo Loconte,Timothy Hospedales,Cristina Cornelio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种神经符号框架，用于本体感知的知识图谱构建，通过结合开放域抽取、基于嵌入的规范化和针对性的LLM修正，提升了知识图谱的一致性并支持符号化查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本图的检索增强生成方法缺乏支持复杂问题所需的符号操作结构，而现有的符号化图方法在使用大语言模型进行知识抽取时容易引入违背常识本体约束的不一致问题。

Method: 提出一种神经符号框架，结合开放域知识抽取、基于嵌入的类型与谓词规范化，以及在后处理阶段使用LLM纠正本体违规，避免重复调用LLM，减少token消耗。

Result: 该方法显著减少了LLM的token使用量，提高了知识图谱的一致性，同时保持了下游问答任务的性能，并验证了所提取的知识图谱适合SPARQL图模式查询。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在保证问答质量的同时，实现了高效、一致且可符号化查询的知识图谱构建，为复杂多跳问答提供了可靠的知识表示基础。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) is a core challenge in AI, particularly for complex queries requiring multi-hop reasoning across documents, or symbolic operations like aggregation or exhaustive listing. Retrieval-augmented generation has become the dominant approach to QA, with recent graph-based variants addressing part of these issues by organizing knowledge to better support compositional questions. However, most textual graph-based RAG methods still lack the structure needed for symbolic operations useful to answer complex questions reliably. This motivates symbolic graph-based approaches, which extract knowledge graphs (KGs) whose relations are logic predicates that enable SQL-like querying. Yet these pipelines typically use LLMs for KG extraction, which can introduce consistency issues, where extracted facts may violate commonsense ontology constraints. We propose a neuro-symbolic framework for ontology-grounded KG construction combining open-domain extraction, embedding-based canonicalization of types and predicates, and targeted LLM-based correction of ontology violations. By deferring corrections to a post-extraction stage, our method avoids repeated LLM calls, substantially reducing token usage while improving KG consistency and preserving downstream QA quality. Finally, we show that the extracted KGs are well suited for symbolic querying by measuring the occurrence of SPARQL graph patterns.

</details>


### [110] [ReasonOps: Operator Segmentation for LLM Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29192)
*Daniel Lee,Owen Queen,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReasonOps是一种无监督、表达力强的方法，用于标注大模型的思维链轨迹，发现不同模型和任务中普遍存在7种重复的推理操作符，这些操作符具有跨模型和领域的通用性，并可用于模型识别、正确性预测和早期质量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分析思维链轨迹的方法要么过于僵化，要么表达能力不足，难以捕捉跨领域和跨模型的特征，因此需要一种更通用、更灵活的分析框架。

Method: 提出ReasonOps方法，通过无监督聚类句子开头的3个token的‘枢纽’来提取普适的推理操作符，并对来自12个LLM、6个模型家族、8个推理基准的44,662条思维链进行分析。

Result: 发现了7个普遍存在的推理操作符（如回溯、推断、假设等），这些操作符在所有模型和任务中均出现；操作符序列可有效识别模型家族（高AUC）；结构特征能预测答案正确性，在AIME等任务上表现优异；并实现了仅用50%轨迹长度就进行高质量的早期预测。

Conclusion: ReasonOps提供了一种通用、无监督的思维链结构分析框架，揭示了大模型推理过程中的共性结构和模型特异性指纹，为理解、评估和优化LLM推理提供了新工具。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought traces from large reasoning models can span tens of thousands of tokens, yet we lack a vocabulary for describing their internal structure. Previous methods developed to analyze chain-of-thought traces are either too rigid or not expressive enough, failing to capture features across domains and models. To remedy this, we develop ReasonOps, an unsupervised, expressive method for annotating chain-of-thought traces, providing succinct universal operators. Using ReasonOps, we analyze 44,662 traces from 12 thinking LLMs spanning 6 families across 8 reasoning benchmarks and discover that they share a common compositional structure: 7 recurring reasoning operators -- discourse-level moves such as backtracking, inferring, and hypothesizing -- that emerge from unsupervised clustering of sentence-initial 3-token pivots. These operators appear across every model family and benchmark domain, confirmed by three independent LLM judges who classify held-out samples at 70 -76% accuracy. We analyze the structure of operators on easy vs. hard problems, revealing that reflective operators are more helpful on hard problems and harm performance on easy problems. Operator sequences are highly model-identifying: a classifier trained on operator distributions alone recovers the source model with macro-AUC, revealing that each model family has a distinctive reasoning fingerprint. Structural operator features predict within-problem answer correctness well above baselines. Classifiers built on these operators reach WP-AUC and on AIME specifically. ReasonOps further enables early quality estimation well before the trace completes: we predict at WP-AUC for only 50% of the trace. The ReasonOps pipeline is unsupervised and annotation-free, enabling deep insights into LLM reasoning traces as well as strong downstream results on model identification and correctness prediction.

</details>


### [111] [Tailoring the Curriculum: Student-Centered Reasoning Distillation via Dynamic Data-Model Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29229)
*Jiahao Huang,Fei Cheng,Junfeng Jiang,Akiko Aizawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了数据-模型兼容性（DMC）指标，用于评估数据集在推理蒸馏中对小模型的适用性，实验表明DMC与蒸馏效果高度相关，且基于DMC动态选择数据可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理蒸馏的效果依赖于训练数据与学生模型的匹配程度，但缺乏有效指标衡量这种匹配性，因此提出DMC来量化数据-模型兼容性。

Method: 提出DMC指标，综合考虑数据质量、相对难度和学生模型能力；通过分析DMC与蒸馏性能的相关性，并以DMC为标准进行数据选择，验证其有效性。

Result: DMC与推理蒸馏性能表现出强相关性；使用DMC筛选数据能显著提升蒸馏效果；动态更新DMC并选择数据可进一步优化性能，且在多种模型和任务上具有一致性。

Conclusion: DMC是一个有效的数据-模型兼容性度量指标，可用于指导推理蒸馏中的高质量数据选择，尤其动态应用DMC能持续提升学生模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation transfers complex reasoning abilities from large language models (LLMs) to smaller ones, yet its success depends on how well the training data align with the student model. This paper introduces the Data-Model Compatibility (DMC) metric, which can be used to assess the suitability of a dataset for reasoning distillation on a student model. DMC provides an assessment by jointly considering data quality, relative difficulty, and student capability. We validated the effectiveness of DMC from two perspectives: (1) DMC exhibits a strong correlation with reasoning distillation performance; and (2) using DMC as the criterion for data selection leads to improved reasoning distillation performance. Both findings are consistently demonstrated across multiple student models and tasks. Moreover, since the DMC of each dataset dynamically changes during training, our experiments demonstrate that dynamically selecting datasets based on DMC can further enhance performance.

</details>


### [112] [Planning with the Views via Scene Self-Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29563)
*Kangrui Wang,Linjie Li,Zhengyuan Yang,Shiqi Chen,Zihan Wang,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Leonidas Guibas,Lijuan Wang,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ViewSuite评测基准，发现当前视觉语言模型（VLMs）在多步视角规划任务中存在推理断层，即虽能理解单步动作对视图的影响，但难以组合多步变换以达成目标视图。为此，作者提出一种结合自我探索与视图图蒸馏的迭代框架，利用探索轨迹构建视图图并蒸馏为多样化监督任务，有效缓解稀疏奖励问题，显著提升模型性能，使Qwen2.5-VL-7B在交互式视角规划任务中从2.5%提升至47.8%，超越GPT-4和Gemini等大模型，展示了自我探索在实现VLMs主动三维空间推理与规划中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型虽然具备一定的视觉-动作理解能力，但在需要多步推理的3D视图规划任务中表现不佳，缺乏系统性评估与改进方法。本文旨在揭示这一“规划断层”问题，并推动VLMs向具备主动空间推理能力的方向发展。

Method: 构建基于ScanNet真实场景的3D点云环境ViewSuite，用于评测VLMs在多步视角规划中的表现；提出一种迭代式自我探索与视图图蒸馏框架：通过多轮探索生成包含成功与失败轨迹的视图图，将图结构知识蒸馏为多种监督任务，重塑模型策略分布，从而克服稀疏奖励问题，提升规划能力。

Result: 在13个前沿VLM上的实验表明，现有模型仅具备基本的单步视图-动作理解，但在多步组合上表现差，且随目标距离增加性能下降明显；所提方法显著提升Qwen2.5-VL-7B在交互式视图规划任务中的成功率，从2.5%提高到47.8%，优于GPT-4.5 Pro（18.5%）和Gemini 3.1 Pro（21.4%）。

Conclusion: 当前VLMs在多步3D视图规划方面存在明显缺陷，单纯依赖预训练不足以支持复杂空间推理；通过自我探索构建视图图并进行知识蒸馏，是一种有效增强VLMs空间规划能力的方法，为未来构建具备主动环境交互能力的智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Can VLMs predict how each camera move changes the view, and plan many such moves ahead? We call this capability view planning, requiring (1)understanding how a single action transforms the view, and (2)composing many such transformations across multi-turn plans to identify a target view. We probe both abilities in our proposed ViewSuite, a 3D point-cloud environment on real ScanNet scenes. Across 13 frontier VLMs, a critical planning gap emerges: they possess basic view-action knowledge but fail to compose it across multi-turn plans, with the gap widening as viewpoint distance grows. To close this gap, we propose an iterative framework that alternates self-exploration with view graph distillation. The key insight is that all exploration trajectories, regardless of their outcome, collectively form a view graph that compactly captures how viewpoints connect across a scene. Distilling this graph into diverse supervised tasks reshapes the policy distribution and overcomes the sparse rewards that stall pure RL. This improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B from 2.5% to 47.8% on interactive view planning, surpassing GPT-5.4 Pro (18.5%) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (21.4%). Self-exploration emerges as a promising path toward VLMs that can actively reason and plan in 3D space.

</details>


### [113] [Surfacing Isolated Learners with Outcome-Independent Mediation of Feedback between Teachers and Students Using AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29240)
*Junsoo Park,Youssef Medhat,Htet Phyo Wai,Ploy Thajchayapong,Ashok K. Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可解释的决策层，通过整合学生学习困难、自我报告与实际表现的差异以及教师未解决的关切，对课程主题进行优先级排序，无需依赖成绩或事后标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI增强的课堂中，尽管有丰富的反馈信号，但难以及时转化为教学决策，尤其是在缺乏成绩或最终结果的情况下。

Method: 结合三种信号：学生学习困难的普遍性、学生自我报告与观察到的困难之间的不一致、教师未解决的关切，生成透明且可解释的主题优先级排序。

Result: 在一门研究生计算机科学课程中，该方法的排序结果与教师关注点高度一致（Spearman ρ=0.80），并与学生报告的难度显著相关（ρ=0.46, p=.048）；多信号融合比单一信号更好地识别出需要帮助的学生（AUC=0.96 vs. 0.91）。

Conclusion: 透明的多信号协调机制有助于在反馈不完整时支持人机协同教学决策，初步结果表明其在促进教学干预方面的潜力。

Abstract: AI-augmented classrooms generate rich teacher and student feedback before graded outcomes become available, yet these signals can be difficult to translate into timely instructional decisions. We propose an interpretable decision layer: a transparent mechanism that ranks course topics requiring attention without using grades or post-hoc outcome labels. The approach combines three signals: student learning difficulty prevalence, disagreement between learner self-reports and observed difficulties, and unresolved teacher concerns. The output is a ranked set of topic priorities with per-topic decision records explaining each ranking. In one graduate CS course offering ($n=5$ instructor interviews; $n=279$ survey responses), prioritized topics aligned with instructor concerns (top-5 overlap 3/5; Spearman $ρ=0.80$) and student-reported topic difficulty ($ρ=0.46$, $p=.048$). Multi-signal integration also surfaced learners not identified through individual signal sources alone (AUC $=0.96$ vs. $0.91$ for gap prevalence alone). Reflective thinking, help-seeking, and self-efficacy provided additional evidence that student behavioral signals align with learning-related constructs. While preliminary, these findings suggest that transparent coordination mechanisms may help support human-AI co-agency when feedback is incomplete.

</details>


### [114] [DenseSteer: Steering Small Language Models towards Dense Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29247)
*Yang Ouyang,Shuhang Lin,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的推理时引导框架DenseSteer，通过促进密集推理模式提升小模型的数学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现较强大的语言模型在数学推理中表现出更少但信息密度更高的推理步骤，而小模型在此方面表现不足，因此提出模拟这种密集推理模式来提升小模型性能。

Method: 基于对Qwen-2.5模型系列的经验分析，提出DenseSteer框架，在推理时通过调节内部表征使其趋向高信息密度的推理路径，无需额外训练。

Result: 实验表明该方法在不增加token级负对数似然的情况下持续提升小模型在数学推理任务上的准确率。

Conclusion: 密集推理是一种有效的结构化解题方式，DenseSteer为提升小模型的复杂推理能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning abilities, while smaller models (<= 3B parameters) significantly underperform on multi-step reasoning tasks. Based on empirical analyses of the Qwen-2.5 model family on math reasoning benchmarks, we find that more proficient reasoning is associated with fewer reasoning steps but higher information density per step, a property we term Dense Reasoning. Motivated by this observation, we propose DenseSteer, a training-free inference-time steering framework that enhances small-model reasoning by modulating internal representations toward dense reasoning patterns. Experiments show that our method yields consistent accuracy improvements without increasing token-level Negative Log-Likelihood, highlighting dense reasoning as an effective structural approach to mathematical problem solving.

</details>


### [115] [When and How Human Curation Backfires: Preference Alignment under Multi-Model Self-Consuming Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29267)
*Yang Zhang,Xiukun Wei,Xueru Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模型自消耗训练范式下的动态系统稳定性及人类干预对模型对齐性的影响，发现跨模型交互可能削弱甚至逆转人工校正的积极作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基础模型越来越多地使用先前模型生成的合成数据进行训练，可能出现模型崩溃、发散或偏见放大等问题。现有研究主要关注单一模型的自我消耗，而实际中多个模型常相互影响，因此需要研究多模型场景下的动态行为和人类干预效果。

Method: 本文首先形式化了一个多模型自消耗交互的框架，并分析其动力系统的收敛性；然后研究了对一个模型进行人工校正如何影响自身（自影响）及其他模型（跨影响）。

Result: 研究表明，在多模型环境下，跨模型交互可能导致人工校正对模型对齐性的提升作用被削弱甚至反转，从而损害长期对齐性。

Conclusion: 在多模型自消耗训练中，传统的仅基于单模型的人类干预策略可能不再有效，需考虑跨模型影响以设计更鲁棒的对齐机制。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly trained on synthetic data generated by prior model iterations rather than exclusively on real data. This self-consuming training paradigm can lead to model collapse, divergence, or bias amplification. Recent work (Ferbach et al., 2024) shows that incorporating human curation into the loop can steer a self-consuming model toward human-aligned behavior, but these analyses focus on a single, isolated model that solely consumes its own outputs. In practice, however, models often interact and train on input-output pairs produced by other models. This paper studies self-consuming training in the multi-model regime.
